# The G-ID-F0m RESULTS entry and Code's escalation block to the seat -- values-check, both passes (before commit).
# Code stamp: 2026-10-13 13:53:27 UTC (date -u). HEAD 33cefe3 (the run's outputs); the entry and the block uncommitted while checked.
# Each pass: three verifiers (the entry's two halves; the block) extract and check every atomic claim with tools; a STRICT
#   refuter re-checks every FAILS/IMPRECISE; a values lens reads both texts whole. All agents read-only; no model loaded;
#   nothing under data/m/ckpt opened (instructed; hashing allowed).
# Pass 1: 257 claims (234 verified, 18 imprecise, 5 uncheckable); refuters 17 CONFIRMED, 1 PLAUSIBLE; 26 values findings
#   (2 major). Pass 2: 275 claims (262 verified, 4 imprecise, 1 fails, 8 uncheckable); refuters 5 CONFIRMED; 22 values
#   findings (3 major; one a clean check). Code's disposition: every confirmed correction, the plausible one (E1-76) and
#   every values finding of both passes applied; Code re-read the sources the findings cite before applying them.

The workflow script (both passes ran it; PASS from args): /Users/jasondwyer/.claude/projects/-Users-jasondwyer-Documents-code-jspace-lab/adb70afc-5886-490b-a675-abb30f8c425f/workflows/scripts/gid-entry-valuescheck-wf_e0275cf3-e22.js
export const meta = {
  name: 'gid-entry-valuescheck',
  description: 'Values-check the G-ID-F0m RESULTS entry and the escalation block to the seat: claim verifiers, strict refuters, a values lens',
  phases: [
    { title: 'Verify', detail: 'three verifiers extract and check every atomic claim' },
    { title: 'Refute', detail: 'a strict refuter per flagged claim batch' },
    { title: 'Values', detail: 'a values lens over both texts' },
  ],
}

const REPO = '/Users/jasondwyer/Documents/code/jspace-lab'
const PY = REPO + '/jlens-qwen36/.venv/bin/python'
const PASS = args && args.pass ? args.pass : 1

const COMMON = `
CONTEXT. Repository ${REPO} (PROPOSAL-m, a pre-registered experiment; Code = the build/run role, the design seat adjudicates, the PI routes). Code has written two texts to check BEFORE commit (values-check pass ${PASS}):
 (E) the new top entry of RESULTS.md, "2026-10-13 — G-ID-F0m ..." (from the line after "## Log — Phase 2" down to the line before the next "- 2026-10-12 — **G-TIME-F0" entry);
 (P) results/raw/2026-10-13_m-code-to-design_G-ID-F0m.md (Code's escalation block to the design seat; uncommitted).
Sources of truth: git history (commits 8da32f2 = the driver scripts/m_sessions.py + results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gid-driver_review.txt + _m-gid-driver_selftest.txt; 33cefe3 = the run's outputs; 27450dc = G-TIME-F0's close with the seat's step-10 read results/raw/2026-10-13_m-design-to-code_G-TIME-F0_read.md; 0fe65b9 = the step-10 driver; e3bef6f = LOCK-m-F0.md v0.1); LOCK-m-F0.md; PROPOSAL-m-gating-campaign-v1_11.md (cite "m:N" = line N); the run raw results/raw/2026-10-13_m-gid-f0m.txt; data/m/gates/Q-F0_G-ID.json; data/m/reports/Q-F0_id_report.json and Q-F0_id_reads.json (G-ID's full records of its sham-only sessions -- reading them is allowed, m:318 exception (4)); data/m/reports/Q-F0_time_report.json (G-TIME-F0's report, for the identity comparison); scripts/m_sessions.py at 8da32f2; valence_core/m/*.py and valence_core/adapters/qwen_mlx.py at HEAD; the gitignored clone ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36/jlens_qwen/*.py (READ ONLY; check its files' sha256 with shasum; never run git inside jlens-qwen36). The PI's message of 2026-10-13 (chat) is quoted verbatim in (E) and in the review raw's header (curly apostrophes as typed) -- treat that quote as given, but check that (E) quotes it identically to the review raw's header and attributes it correctly (the PI's routing; the read's words are the seat's). Code's session-only facts (pgrep, find, pmset power 140 W, df 479 GiB free, the dry precheck, the self-test runs, the final mutants) are stated in (E), the raw's header and the review/selftest raws; check them against those committed raws where they appear, and say UNCHECKABLE (session-only) otherwise.
HARD RULES: read-only. Do NOT edit any file. No git command that writes. Never run git inside ${REPO}/jlens-qwen36. Do NOT load a model and do NOT run the driver's stages or self-test (no python process whose command line names mlx, jlens, qwen, olmo or ipykernel is needed; plain python3 over JSON is fine; ${PY} only with -I -c for read-only checks that need valence_core). NEVER open, print, parse or read data/m/ckpt/* (pick-bearing; hashing it with shasum is allowed).`

const CLAIMS = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: {
    claims: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
      id: { type: 'string' }, text: { type: 'string' }, where: { type: 'string' },
      verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['VERIFIED', 'FAILS', 'IMPRECISE', 'UNCHECKABLE'] },
      evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' },
    }, required: ['id', 'text', 'where', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } },
  },
  required: ['claims'],
}
const REF = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { verdicts: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, verdict: { type: 'string', enum: ['CONFIRMED', 'REFUTED', 'PLAUSIBLE'] },
    evidence: { type: 'string' }, correction: { type: 'string' },
  }, required: ['id', 'verdict', 'evidence', 'correction'] } } },
  required: ['verdicts'],
}
const VALUES = {
  type: 'object',
  properties: { findings: { type: 'array', items: { type: 'object', properties: {
    id: { type: 'string' }, severity: { type: 'string', enum: ['major', 'minor', 'note'] },
    where: { type: 'string' }, finding: { type: 'string' }, suggested_text: { type: 'string' },
  }, required: ['id', 'severity', 'where', 'finding', 'suggested_text'] } } },
  required: ['findings'],
}

const PARTS = [
  { key: 'E1', scope: 'text (E) from its first line through the end of the "Launch conditions" bullet (the headline, its sub-bullets, the stamp, the close commit, the word, the driver -- sha, what it does, the stage-generic path, the offline load, the PIN sites and their line numbers at 8da32f2 and 0fe65b9, the review and its counts, the self-test -- and the launch conditions, the offline load and the clone hashes)' },
  { key: 'E2', scope: 'text (E) from "The run" bullet to its end (the run lines, identity, pairs, divergence, the kernel-split disclosure, reported beside, timing, the outputs table with sha256s, carried items, the ping summary, what follows)' },
  { key: 'P', scope: 'text (P), the whole block (short form, sections 1-5; its quotes of m:267, m:281, m:282, m:318 and the reads file; every code anchor file:line in section 3 against the actual files; the questions and Code\'s stated defaults). Check the pair-1 uncached transcript in section 2 is byte-identical to the "transcript" field of the reads session with j=1, path uncached, and that the cached excerpt matches the cached session\'s transcript tail; check the round-by-round comparison claims against the reads\' "rounds"' },
]

const results = await pipeline(
  PARTS,
  p => agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (verifier ${p.key}, pass ${PASS}). Extract EVERY atomic factual claim in ${p.scope} -- every number, hash, path, commit, line anchor, quote, attribution, count, date/stamp, and every statement about what code does or what a ruling/word says -- and check each against the sources with tools (git show/log/grep, shasum, reading files, read-only python3 over JSON). Quotes must be verbatim (say exactly where they differ). Attributions must be exact (who said it; whose reading). Verdict per claim: VERIFIED, FAILS (wrong), IMPRECISE (true in part / overstated / misattributed), UNCHECKABLE (say why). Give the evidence and, for FAILS/IMPRECISE, an exact correction. Be exhaustive: a missed claim is a failure of this pass.`,
    { label: `verify:${p.key}`, phase: 'Verify', schema: CLAIMS }),
  (res, p) => {
    const flagged = (res && res.claims ? res.claims : []).filter(c => c.verdict === 'FAILS' || c.verdict === 'IMPRECISE')
    if (flagged.length === 0) return { part: p.key, claims: res ? res.claims : [], refuted: [] }
    return agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (STRICT refuter, ${p.key}, pass ${PASS}). A verifier flagged the claims below as FAILS or IMPRECISE. For EACH, independently re-check against the sources and try to REFUTE the flag (i.e. show the original claim is in fact right). CONFIRMED = the flag stands (the text must change; give the exact corrected wording, minimal, preserving everything true); REFUTED = the claim was right (say why); PLAUSIBLE = unsettled.
FLAGGED:
${JSON.stringify(flagged, null, 1)}`, { label: `refute:${p.key}`, phase: 'Refute', schema: REF })
      .then(v => ({ part: p.key, claims: res.claims, refuted: v ? v.verdicts : [] }))
  }
)
const values = await agent(`${COMMON}
TASK (values lens, pass ${PASS}). Read (E) and (P) whole, as the design seat and the PI will. Find what a careful reader would object to that a fact check misses: misattribution of readings (Code's vs the seat's vs the PI's vs the proposal's; e.g. is anything stated as m:281's or the seat's that is only Code's reading?); any statement that self-adjudicates what is the seat's to decide (G-ID FAILED on a hair trigger; the consequence is posed to the seat as Q1 with Code's default -- check neither text treats it as decided, nor attributes the divergence to a cause); any leak against the blinding rules (m:271, m:318: no contrast pick, no contrast free-round class or position, no contrast timing; G-ID is sham-only and its full reads are m:318 exception (4); nothing from data/m/ckpt may be stated); overclaims ("all", "every", "nothing", "none", "identical"); questions to the seat that are not neutrally posed or that pre-decide; the read's step-12 terms and the PI's word ("its sha256 recorded in the G-ID-F0m entry and in v0.2"; "offline load with the snapshot id in the raw's header and the entry"; "the checklist's own launch line, any departure stated in the header at the time"; Q6: "G-ID-F0m's sham seconds on both paths when they exist" beside the bases) -- check each is met and said accurately; internal inconsistencies between (E), (P) and the raw's header; anything unclear to a reader without Code's session. Give each finding with suggested replacement text.`,
  { label: 'values', phase: 'Values', schema: VALUES })
return { pass: PASS, parts: results, values: values ? values.findings : [] }
